      "content": "package QuantCommon\n\nobject FpBackend {\n  sealed trait Mode\n  case object HardFloatCompat extends Mode\n  case object VivadoIp extends Mode\n\n  private var current: Mode = HardFloatCompat\n\n  def mode: Mode = current\n  def useVivadoIp: Boolean = current == VivadoIp\n\n  def setHardFloatCompat(): Unit = {\n    current = HardFloatCompat\n  }\n\n  def setVivadoIp(): Unit = {\n    current = VivadoIp\n  }\n}\n",

      "content": "package QuantCommon\n\nimport QuantCommon.Precision._\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.experimental.ExtModule\nimport chisel3.util.{Cat, ShiftRegister}\nimport hardfloat._\n\nobject XilinxFpTargetConfig {\n  // Vivado 2021.1 floating_point IP, single precision, Speed_Optimized,\n  // Maximum_Latency=true, Flow_Control=Blocking\n  val AddLatency = 12\n  val MulLatency = 9\n  val DivLatency = 29\n  val SqrtLatency = 29\n  val CompareLatency = 3\n  val FixedToFloatLatency = 7\n  val FloatToFixedLatency = 7\n\n  // HardFloat DivSqrtRecFN_small uses sigWidth + 3 cycles for FP32.\n  val HardFloatDivSqrtLatency = FP32_SIG_WIDTH + 3\n  val DivSqrtPadLatency = DivLatency - HardFloatDivSqrtLatency\n}\n\nprivate object XilinxFpCompatUtil {\n  def keep[T <: Data](x: T): T = {\n    dontTouch(x)\n    x\n  }\n\n  def axisByteWidth(bits: Int): Int = ((bits + 7) / 8) * 8\n\n  def delayValid(in: Bool, stages: Int): Bool = {\n    if (stages == 0) in\n    else {\n      val regs = RegInit(VecInit(Seq.fill(stages)(false.B)))\n      regs(0) := in\n      for (i <- 1 until stages) {\n        regs(i) := regs(i - 1)\n      }\n      regs(stages - 1)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nprivate object XilinxFpIpName {\n  def add(subOp: Boolean): String = if (subOp) \"fp_sub_sp_12\" else \"fp_add_sp_12\"\n  val mul = \"fp_mul_sp_9\"\n  def compare(kind: String): String = s\"fp_cmp_${kind}_sp_3\"\n  def intToFloat(intWidth: Int, signedIn: Boolean): String =\n    s\"fp_i2f_${if (signedIn) \"s\" else \"u\"}${intWidth}_sp_7\"\n  def floatToInt(intWidth: Int, signedOut: Boolean): String =\n    s\"fp_f2i_${if (signedOut) \"s\" else \"u\"}${intWidth}_sp_7\"\n  val div = \"fp_div_sp_29\"\n  val sqrt = \"fp_sqrt_sp_29\"\n}\n\nprivate class VivadoFpBinaryIp(moduleName: String, resultWidth: Int = FP32_WIDTH) extends ExtModule {\n  override def desiredName: String = moduleName\n  val aclk = IO(Input(Clock()))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tdata = IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W)))\n  val s_axis_b_tvalid = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_b_tready = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_b_tdata = IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W)))\n  val m_axis_result_tvalid = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val m_axis_result_tready = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = IO(Output(UInt(resultWidth.W)))\n}\n\nprivate class VivadoFpBinaryIpNoResultReady(moduleName: String, resultWidth: Int = FP32_WIDTH) extends ExtModule {\n  override def desiredName: String = moduleName\n  val aclk = IO(Input(Clock()))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tdata = IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W)))\n  val s_axis_b_tvalid = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_b_tready = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_b_tdata = IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W)))\n  val m_axis_result_tvalid = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = IO(Output(UInt(resultWidth.W)))\n}\n\nprivate class VivadoFpUnaryIp(moduleName: String, inWidth: Int, resultWidth: Int) extends ExtModule {\n  override def desiredName: String = moduleName\n  val aclk = IO(Input(Clock()))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tdata = IO(Input(UInt(inWidth.W)))\n  val m_axis_result_tvalid = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val m_axis_result_tready = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = IO(Output(UInt(resultWidth.W)))\n}\n\nprivate class VivadoFpUnaryIpNoResultReady(moduleName: String, inWidth: Int, resultWidth: Int) extends ExtModule {\n  override def desiredName: String = moduleName\n  val aclk = IO(Input(Clock()))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tdata = IO(Input(UInt(inWidth.W)))\n  val m_axis_result_tvalid = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = IO(Output(UInt(resultWidth.W)))\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatAddCore(val subOpValue: Boolean) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val a = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val b = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val out = Output(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n  })\n\n  val add = Module(new AddRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH))\n  add.io.subOp := subOpValue.B\n  add.io.a := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.a)\n  add.io.b := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.b)\n  add.io.roundingMode := ROUNDING_MODE\n  add.io.detectTininess := DETECT_TININESS.asBool\n  io.out := fNFromRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, add.io.out)\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatMulCore extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val a = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val b = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val out = Output(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n  })\n\n  val mul = Module(new MulRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH))\n  mul.io.a := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.a)\n  mul.io.b := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.b)\n  mul.io.roundingMode := ROUNDING_MODE\n  mul.io.detectTininess := DETECT_TININESS.asBool\n  io.out := fNFromRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, mul.io.out)\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatCompareCore extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val a = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val b = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val lt = Output(Bool())\n    val eq = Output(Bool())\n    val gt = Output(Bool())\n  })\n\n  val cmp = Module(new CompareRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH))\n  cmp.io.a := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.a)\n  cmp.io.b := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.b)\n  cmp.io.signaling := false.B\n  io.lt := cmp.io.lt\n  io.eq := cmp.io.eq\n  io.gt := cmp.io.gt\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatIntToFpCore(val intWidth: Int, val signedIn: Boolean) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val in = Input(UInt(intWidth.W))\n    val out = Output(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n  })\n\n  val cvt = Module(new INToRecFN(intWidth, FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH))\n  cvt.io.signedIn := signedIn.B\n  cvt.io.in := io.in\n  cvt.io.roundingMode := ROUNDING_MODE\n  cvt.io.detectTininess := DETECT_TININESS\n  io.out := fNFromRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, cvt.io.out)\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatFpToIntCore(val intWidth: Int, val signedOut: Boolean) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val in = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val out = Output(UInt(intWidth.W))\n  })\n\n  val cvt = Module(new RecFNToIN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, intWidth))\n  cvt.io.in := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.in)\n  cvt.io.roundingMode := ROUNDING_MODE\n  cvt.io.signedOut := signedOut.B\n  io.out := cvt.io.out\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatDivSqrtCore(val sqrtOpValue: Boolean) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val inValid = Input(Bool())\n    val inReady = Output(Bool())\n    val a = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val b = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val outValid = Output(Bool())\n    val out = Output(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n  })\n\n  val divSqrt = Module(new DivSqrtRecFN_small(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, 0))\n  divSqrt.io.inValid := io.inValid\n  divSqrt.io.sqrtOp := sqrtOpValue.B\n  divSqrt.io.a := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.a)\n  divSqrt.io.b := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.b)\n  divSqrt.io.roundingMode := ROUNDING_MODE\n  divSqrt.io.detectTininess := DETECT_TININESS\n  io.inReady := divSqrt.io.inReady\n  io.outValid := Mux(sqrtOpValue.B, divSqrt.io.outValid_sqrt, divSqrt.io.outValid_div)\n  io.out := fNFromRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, divSqrt.io.out)\n}\n\nclass XilinxFpAddCompat(val subOp: Boolean = false) extends Module {\n  val aclk = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Clock())))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_a_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))))\n  val s_axis_b_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_b_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_b_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))))\n  val m_axis_result_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val m_axis_result_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))))\n\n  if (FpBackend.useVivadoIp) {\n    val ip = Module(new VivadoFpBinaryIpNoResultReady(XilinxFpIpName.add(subOp)))\n    ip.aclk := aclk\n    ip.s_axis_a_tvalid := s_axis_a_tvalid\n    ip.s_axis_a_tdata := s_axis_a_tdata\n    ip.s_axis_b_tvalid := s_axis_b_tvalid\n    ip.s_axis_b_tdata := s_axis_b_tdata\n    s_axis_a_tready := ip.s_axis_a_tready\n    s_axis_b_tready := ip.s_axis_b_tready\n    m_axis_result_tvalid := ip.m_axis_result_tvalid\n    m_axis_result_tdata := ip.m_axis_result_tdata\n  } else {\n    withClockAndReset(aclk, reset) {\n      val core = Module(new HardFloatAddCore(subOp))\n      val inFire = s_axis_a_tvalid && s_axis_b_tvalid\n      core.io.a := s_axis_a_tdata\n      core.io.b := s_axis_b_tdata\n      s_axis_a_tready := true.B\n      s_axis_b_tready := true.B\n      m_axis_result_tvalid := XilinxFpMulCompat extends Module {\n  val aclk = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Clock())))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = ip.s_axis_a_tvalid := s_axis_a_tvalid\n    ip.s_axis_a_tdata := s_axis_a_tdata\n    ip.s_axis_b_tvalid := s_axis_b_tvalid\n    ip.s_axis_b_tdata := s_axis_b_tdata\n    s_axis_a_tready := ip.s_axis_a_tready\n    s_axis_b_tready := ip.s_axis_b_tready\n    m_axis_result_tvalid := ip.m_axis_result_tvalid\n    m_axis_result_tdata := ip.m_axis_result_tdata\n  } else {\n    withClockAndReset(aclk, reset) {\n      val core = Module(new HardFloatMulCore)\n      val inFire = s_axis_a_tvalid && s_axis_b_tvalid\n      core.io.a := s_axis_a_tdata\n      core.io.b := s_axis_b_tdata\n      s_axis_a_tready := true.B\n      s_axis_b_tready := true.B\n      m_axis_result_tvalid := XilinxFpCompatUtil.delayValid(inFire, XilinxFpTargetConfig.MulLatency)\n      m_axis_result_tdata := ShiftRegister(core.io.out, XilinxFpTargetConfig.MulLatency)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nclass XilinxFpCompareCompat(val compareKind: String) extends Module {\n  val aclk = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Clock())))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_a_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))))\n  val s_axis_b_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_b_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_b_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))))\n  val m_axis_result_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val m_axis_result_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(UInt(8.W))))\n\n  if (FpBackend.useVivadoIp) {\n    val ip = Module(new VivadoFpBinaryIpNoResultReady(XilinxFpIpName.compare(compareKind), resultWidth = 8))\n    ip.aclk := aclk\n    ip.s_axis_a_tvalid := s_axis_a_tvalid\n    ip.s_axis_a_tdata := inSigWidth)\n        io.exceptionFlags := isSigNaNRawFloat(rawIn) ## 0.U(4.W)\n\n    } else {\n\n        //--------------------------------------------------------------------\n        //--------------------------------------------------------------------\n        val roundAnyRawFNToRecFN =\n            Module(\n                new RoundAnyRawFNToRecFN(\n                        inExpWidth,\n                        inSigWidth,\n                        outExpWidth,\n                        outSigWidth,\n                        flRoundOpt_sigMSBitAlwaysZero\n                    ))\n        roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.invalidExc     := isSigNaNRawFloat(rawIn)\n        roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.infiniteExc    := false.B\n        roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.in             := rawIn\n        roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.roundingMode   := io.roundingMode\n        roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.detectTininess := io.detectTininess\n        io.out            := roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.out\n        io.exceptionFlags := roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.exceptionFlags\n    }\n}\n\n",

      "content": "\n/*============================================================================\n\nThis Chisel source file is part of a pre-release version of the HardFloat IEEE\nFloating-Point Arithmetic Package, by John R. Neither the name of the University nor the names of its contributors may\n    be used to endorse or promote products derived from this software without\n    specific prior written permission.\n\nTHIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY THE REGENTS AND CONTRIBUTORS \"AS IS\", AND ANY\nEXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, THE IMPLIED\nWARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE, ARE\nDISCLAIMED.  IN NO EVENT SHALL THE REGENTS OR CONTRIBUTORS BE LIABLE FOR ANY\nDIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES\n(INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES;\nLOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND\nON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT\n(INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE) ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS\nSOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.\n\n=============================================================================*/\n\npackage hardfloat\n\nimport chisel3._\n\nobject recFNFromFN\n{\n    def apply(expWidth: Int, sigWidth: Int, in: Bits) =\n    {\n        val rawIn = rawFloatFromFN(expWidth, sigWidth, in)\n        rawIn.sign ##\n          (Mux(rawIn.isZero, 0.U(3.W), rawIn.sExp(expWidth, expWidth - 2)) |\n                Mux(rawIn.isNaN, 1.U, 0.U)) ##\n            rawIn.sExp(expWidth - 3, 0) ##\n            rawIn.sig(sigWidth - 2, 0)\n    }\n}\n\n",

      "content": "\n/*============================================================================\n\nThis Chisel source file is part of a pre-release version of the HardFloat IEEE\nFloating-Point Arithmetic Package, by John R. Neither the name of the University nor the names of its contributors may\n    be used to endorse or promote products derived from this software without\n    specific prior written permission.\n\nTHIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY THE REGENTS AND CONTRIBUTORS \"AS IS\", AND ANY\nEXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, THE IMPLIED\nWARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE, ARE\nDISCLAIMED.  IN NO EVENT SHALL THE REGENTS OR CONTRIBUTORS BE LIABLE FOR ANY\nDIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES\n(INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES;\nLOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND\nON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT\n(INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE) ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS\nSOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.\n\n=============================================================================*/\n\npackage hardfloat\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nobject fNFromRecFN\n{\n    def apply(expWidth: Int, sigWidth: Int, in: Bits) =\n    {\n        val minNormExp = (BigInt(1)<<(expWidth - 1)) + 2\n\n        val rawIn = rawFloatFromRecFN(expWidth, sigWidth, in)\n\n        val isSubnormal = rawIn.sExp < minNormExp.S\n        val denormShiftDist = 1.U - rawIn.sExp(log2Up(sigWidth - 1) - 1, 0)\n        val denormFract = ((rawIn.sig>>1)>>denormShiftDist)(sigWidth - 2, 0)\n\n        val expOut =\n            Mux(isSubnormal,\n                0.U,\n                rawIn.sExp(expWidth - 1, 0) -\n                  ((BigInt(1)<<(expWidth - 1)) + 1).U\n            ) | Fill(expWidth, rawIn.isNaN || rawIn.isInf)\n        val fractOut =\n            Mux(isSubnormal,\n                denormFract,\n                Mux(rawIn.isInf, 0.U, rawIn.sig(sigWidth - 2, 0))\n            )\n        Cat(rawIn.sign, expOut, fractOut)\n    }\n}\n\n",
      "path": "accagent/runs/spatialacc_qwen_agent_fast_run/generated/chisel/src/main/scala/hardfloat/fNFromRecFN.scala",

      "bytes": 5387,
      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\nimport chisel3.util.experimental.loadMemoryFromFileInline\nimport hardfloat._\nimport hardfloat.consts\n\nfinal case class ActivationParams(\n  hiddenSize: Int,\n  lanes: Int = 12,\n  elemBits: Int = 8,\n  batchSize: Int = 16,\n  maxSeqLen: Int = 16,\n  kind: String = \"relu\"\n) {\n  require(hiddenSize % lanes == 0, \"hiddenSize must be divisible by lanes\")\n  val beats: Int = hiddenSize / lanes\n  val beatBits: Int = lanes * elemBits\n  val addrBits: Int = log2Ceil(batchSize * beats max 2)\n}\n\nclass Activation(p: ActivationParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.beatBits, p.addrBits))))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.beatBits, p.addrBits)))\n  })\n\n  private val SigmoidTablePath =\n    \"src/main/resources/spatialaccagent/numeric/sigmoid_pwl_q18.memh\"\n  private val Fp32ExpWidth = 8\n  private val Fp32SigWidth = 24\n  private val Q11Width = 16\n  private val SigmoidFractionBits = 18\n\n  private def fp32ToSignedQ11(value: UInt): SInt = {\n    val scaled = IeeeMath.mulFp32(value, IeeeMath.fp32Constant(2048.0))\n    val convert = Module(new RecFNToIN(Fp32ExpWidth, Fp32SigWidth, Q11Width))\n    convert.io.in := recFNFromFN(Fp32ExpWidth, Fp32SigWidth, scaled)\n    convert.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n    convert.io.signedOut := true.B\n    convert.io.out.asSInt\n  }\n\n  private def signedIntegerToFp32(value: SInt): UInt = {\n    val convert = Module(new INToRecFN(value.getWidth, Fp32ExpWidth, Fp32SigWidth))\n    convert.io.signedIn := true.B\n    convert.io.in := value.asUInt\n    convert.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n    convert.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n    fNFromRecFN(Fp32ExpWidth, Fp32SigWidth, convert.io.out)\n  }\n\n  io.in.ready := io.out.ready\n  io.out.valid := io.in.valid\n  io.out.bits.st := io.in.bits.st\n  io.out.bits.addr := io.in.bits.addr\n  io.out.bits.last := io.in.bits.last\n\n  val inVec = io.in.bits.data.asTypeOf(Vec(p.lanes, SInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val outVec = Wire(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n\n  if (p.kind == \"silu\" || p.kind == \"swish\") {\n    require(\n      p.elemBits == 16 || p.elemBits == 32,\n      \"target-model SiLU requires IEEE fp16 or fp32 stream elements\"\n    )\n\n    val sigmoidTable = Mem(129, UInt(19.W))\n    loadMemoryFromFileInline(sigmoidTable, SigmoidTablePath)\n\n    val minusEight = IeeeMath.fp32Constant(-8.0)\n    val plusEight = IeeeMath.fp32Constant(8.0)\n    val q18One = (BigInt(1) << SigmoidFractionBits).U(19.W)\n    val productScale = IeeeMath.fp32Constant(math.pow(2.0, -29.0))\n\n    for (i <- 0 until p.lanes) {\n      val inputBits = inVec(i).asUInt\n      val inputFp32 = IeeeMath.toFp32(inputBits, p.elemBits)\n      val atOrBelowMinusEight = !IeeeMath.lessThanFp32(minusEight, inputFp32)\n      val atOrAbovePlusEight = !IeeeMath.lessThanFp32(inputFp32, plusEight)\n      val inputQ11 = fp32ToSignedQ11(inputFp32)\n\n      val tableInputQ11 = Wire(SInt(Q11Width.W))\n      tableInputQ11 := Mux(\n        atOrBelowMinusEight,\n        (-16384).S(Q11Width.W),\n        Mux(atOrAbovePlusEight, 16383.S(Q11Width.W), inputQ11)\n      )\n\n      val biasedQ11 = (tableInputQ11.pad(18) + 16384.S(18.W)).asUInt\n      val segment = biasedQ11(14, 8)\n      val remainder = biasedQ11(7, 0)\n      val nextSegment = segment +& 1.U\n      val tableBase = sigmoidTable.read(segment)\n      val tableNext = sigmoidTable.read(nextSegment)\n      val tableDelta = tableNext.zext - tableBase.zext\n      val remainderSigned = Cat(0.U(1.W), remainder).asSInt\n      val interpolationProduct = tableDelta * remainderSigned\n      val roundedDelta =\n        (interpolationProduct + 128.S(interpolationProduct.getWidth.W)) >> 8\n      val interpolatedWide = tableBase.zext + roundedDelta\n      val interpolatedBits = interpolatedWide.asUInt\n      val interpolatedQ18 = interpolatedBits(18, 0)\n\n      val sigmoidQ18 = Wire(UInt(19.W))\n      sigmoidQ18 := Mux(\n        atOrBelowMinusEight,\n        0.U,\n        Mux(atOrAbovePlusEight, q18One, interpolatedQ18)\n      )\n\n      val siluProductQ29 = inputQ11 * sigmoidQ18.zext\n      val productFp32 = signedIntegerToFp32(siluProductQ29)\n      val siluFp32 = IeeeMath.mulFp32(productFp32, productScale)\n      val boundedSilu = Mux(\n        atOrBelowMinusEight,\n        IeeeMath.fp32Zero,\n        Mux(atOrAbovePlusEight, inputFp32, siluFp32)\n      )\n      outVec(i) := IeeeMath.fromFp32(boundedSilu, p.elemBits)\n    }\n  } else {\n    for (i <- 0 until p.lanes) {\n      val x = inVec(i)\n      val y =\n        if (p.kind == \"relu\") {\n          Mux(x < 0.S, 0.S(p.elemBits.W), x)\n        } else if (\n          p.kind == \"gelu\" ||\n          p.kind == \"gelu_new\" ||\n          p.kind == \"gelu_pytorch_tanh\"\n        ) {\n          Mux(x < 0.S, (x >> 1).asSInt, x)\n        } else {\n          x\n        }\n      outVec(i) := AccMath.resizeSignedToUInt(y, p.elemBits)\n    }\n  }\n\n  io.out.bits.data := outVec.asUInt\n}\n\nclass ActivationReLU(p: ActivationParams) extends Activation(p.copy(kind = \"relu\"))\nclass ActivationGELUNew(p: ActivationParams) extends Activation(p.copy(kind = \"gelu_new\"))\nclass ActivationSiLU(p: ActivationParams) extends Activation(p.copy(kind = \"silu\"))\nclass ActivationGELUTanh(p: ActivationParams) extends Activation(p.copy(kind = \"gelu_pytorch_tanh\"))\n",
      "path": "accagent/framework/templates/operator_chisel/Activation.scala",
      "sha256": "117a794a9f1217d330de09a0921583750d7fe1cb001cf2847eaf90d752a6e146",
      "truncated": false
    },

      "bytes": 9097,
      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class AttentionParams(\n  hiddenSize: Int,\n  qHeads: Int,\n  kvHeads: Int,\n  headDim: Int,\n  seqLen: Int,\n  lanes: Int = 8,\n  elemBits: Int = 16,\n  qkvElemsPerBeat: Int = 8,\n  batchSize: Int = 16\n) {\n  require(hiddenSize == qHeads * headDim, \"attention hidden size must equal qHeads * headDim\")\n  require(qHeads > 0 && kvHeads > 0 && qHeads % kvHeads == 0, \"invalid GQA head grouping\")\n  require(headDim > 0 && seqLen > 0, \"attention dimensions must be positive\")\n  require(elemBits == 16, \"the immutable attention internal element type is IEEE FP16\")\n  require(hiddenSize % lanes == 0, \"hiddenSize must be divisible by output lanes\")\n\n  val qkv: QKVProjectionParams = QKVProjectionParams(\n    hiddenSize,\n    qHeads,\n    kvHeads,\n    headDim,\n    lanes = lanes,\n    elemBits = elemBits,\n    qkvElemsPerBeat = qkvElemsPerBeat,\n    batchSize = batchSize,\n    maxSeqLen = seqLen\n  )\n  val qDim: Int = qHeads * headDim\n  val kDim: Int = kvHeads * headDim\n  val kvGroupSize: Int = qHeads / kvHeads\n  val seqBits: Int = log2Ceil(seqLen + 1 max 2)\n  val rowBits: Int = log2Ceil(seqLen max 2)\n  val headVectorBits: Int = headDim * elemBits\n  val headAddrBits: Int = log2Ceil(qHeads max 2)\n  val softmax: SoftmaxParams = SoftmaxParams(\n    seqLen = seqLen,\n    lanes = seqLen,\n    elemBits = 32,\n    batchSize = seqLen * qHeads,\n    causal = true,\n    slidingWindow = 0\n  )\n  val outLinear: LinearParams = LinearParams(\n    inDim = qDim,\n    outDim = hiddenSize,\n    inLanes = headDim,\n    outLanes = lanes,\n    elemBits = elemBits,\n    accBits = 32,\n    outputBits = 32,\n    batchSize = seqLen,\n    maxSeqLen = seqLen,\n    hasBias = false\n  )\n  val addrBits: Int = outLinear.outputAddrBits\n}\n\nclass Attention(p: AttentionParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(p.seqBits))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new QKVStreamBeat(p.qkv)))\n    val mask = Input(UInt(p.seqLen.W))\n    val scoreScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n    val ctxInvScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n    val ctxZeroPoint = Input(UInt(8.W))\n    val outInvScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n    val outWeight = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.outLinear.weightTileBits, p.outLinear.weightAddrBits)))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.outLinear.outputBeatBits, p.outLinear.outputAddrBits)))\n  })\n\n  val qMemory = Reg(Vec(p.seqLen * p.qDim, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val kMemory = Reg(Vec(p.seqLen * p.kDim, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val vMemory = Reg(Vec(p.seqLen * p.kDim, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val contextMemory = Reg(Vec(p.seqLen * p.qDim, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n\n  val seqLimit = RegInit(p.seqLen.U(p.seqBits.W))\n  val normalizedSeq = Mux(\n    io.cfg.seqlen === 0.U || io.cfg.seqlen > p.seqLen.U,\n    p.seqLen.U(p.seqBits.W),\n    io.cfg.seqlen\n  )\n  when(io.cfgValid) {\n    seqLimit := normalizedSeq\n  }\n  val activeSeq = Mux(io.cfgValid, normalizedSeq, seqLimit)\n\n  val sCollect :: sScore :: sProbability :: sProjectFeed :: sProjectDrain :: Nil = Enum(5)\n  val state = RegInit(sCollect)\n  val collectToken = RegInit(0.U(p.rowBits.W))\n  val workRow = RegInit(0.U(p.rowBits.W))\n  val workHead = RegInit(0.U(p.qkv.headBits.W))\n  val projectToken = RegInit(0.U(p.rowBits.W))\n  val projectBeat = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.qHeads max 2).W))\n\n  io.in.ready := state === sCollect\n  val inputFields = io.in.bits.data.asTypeOf(Vec(3 * p.qkvElemsPerBeat, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val inputKvHead = io.in.bits.head / p.kvGroupSize.U\n\n  when(io.in.fire) {\n    for (lane <- 0 until p.qkvElemsPerBeat) {\n      val dimension = io.in.bits.addr * p.qkvElemsPerBeat.U + lane.U\n      val qIndex = collectToken * p.qDim.U + io.in.bits.head * p.headDim.U + dimension\n      val kvIndex = collectToken * p.kDim.U + inputKvHead * p.headDim.U + dimension\n      qMemory(qIndex) := inputFields(lane)\n      kMemory(kvIndex) := inputFields(p.qkvElemsPerBeat + lane)\n      vMemory(kvIndex) := inputFields(2 * p.qkvElemsPerBeat + lane)\n    }\n\n    when(io.in.bits.last) {\n      when(collectToken === activeSeq - 1.U) {\n        collectToken := 0.U\n        workRow := 0.U\n        workHead := 0.U\n        state := sScore\n      }.otherwise {\n        collectToken := collectToken + 1.U\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  val attentionScale = IeeeMath.fp32Constant(math.pow(p.headDim.toDouble, -0.5))\n  val scoreValues = Wire(Vec(p.seqLen, UInt(32.W)))\n  val selectedKvHead = workHead / p.kvGroupSize.U\n\n  for (keyToken <- 0 until p.seqLen) {\n    val products = (0 until p.headDim).map { dimension =>\n      val qIndex = workRow * p.qDim.U + workHead * p.headDim.U + dimension.U\n      val kIndex = keyToken.U * p.kDim.U + selectedKvHead * p.headDim.U + dimension.U\n      IeeeMath.mulFp32(\n        IeeeMath.toFp32(qMemory(qIndex), p.elemBits),\n        IeeeMath.toFp32(kMemory(kIndex), p.elemBits)\n      )\n    }\n    scoreValues(keyToken) := IeeeMath.mulFp32(IeeeMath.sumFp32(products), attentionScale)\n  }\n\n  val softmax = Module(new Softmax(p.softmax))\n  val activeMaskBits = Wire(Vec(p.seqLen, Bool()))\n  for (token <- 0 until p.seqLen) {\n    activeMaskBits(token) := token.U < seqLimit\n  }\n\n  softmax.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  softmax.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  softmax.io.rowIndex := workRow\n  softmax.io.mask := activeMaskBits.asUInt\n  softmax.io.in.valid := state === sScore\n  softmax.io.in.bits.data := scoreValues.asUInt\n  softmax.io.in.bits.st := workRow === 0.U && workHead === 0.U\n  softmax.io.in.bits.addr := workRow * p.qHeads.U + workHead\n  softmax.io.in.bits.last := true.B\n  softmax.io.out.ready := state === sProbability\n\n  when(softmax.io.in.fire) {\n    state := sProbability\n  }\n\n  val probabilities = softmax.io.out.bits.data.asTypeOf(Vec(p.seqLen, UInt(32.W)))\n  val contextValues = Wire(Vec(p.headDim, UInt(32.W)))\n  for (dimension <- 0 until p.headDim) {\n    val products = (0 until p.seqLen).map { valueToken =>\n      val vIndex = valueToken.U * p.kDim.U + selectedKvHead * p.headDim.U + dimension.U\n      IeeeMath.mulFp32(\n        probabilities(valueToken),\n        IeeeMath.toFp32(vMemory(vIndex), p.elemBits)\n      )\n    }\n    contextValues(dimension) := IeeeMath.sumFp32(products)\n  }\n\n  when(softmax.io.out.fire) {\n    for (dimension <- 0 until p.headDim) {\n      val contextIndex = workRow * p.qDim.U + workHead * p.headDim.U + dimension.U\n      contextMemory(contextIndex) := IeeeMath.fromFp32(contextValues(dimension), p.elemBits)\n    }\n\n    when(workHead === (p.qHeads - 1).U) {\n      workHead := 0.U\n      when(workRow === seqLimit - 1.U) {\n        workRow := 0.U\n        projectToken := 0.U\n        projectBeat := 0.U\n        state := sProjectFeed\n      }.otherwise {\n        workRow := workRow + 1.U\n        state := sScore\n      }\n    }.otherwise {\n      workHead := workHead + 1.U\n      state := sScore\n    }\n  }\n\n  val outputProjection = Module(new Linear(p.outLinear))\n  outputProjection.io.start := false.B\n  outputProjection.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  outputProjection.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  outputProjection.io.weight <> io.outWeight\n  outputProjection.io.bias.valid := false.B\n  outputProjection.io.bias.bits.addr := 0.U\n  outputProjection.io.bias.bits.data := 0.U\n  outputProjection.io.scale.outScale := IeeeMath.fp32One\n  outputProjection.io.scale.biasScale := IeeeMath.fp32One\n\n  val projectionInput = Wire(Vec(p.headDim, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  for (dimension <- 0 until p.headDim) {\n    val contextIndex = projectToken * p.qDim.U + projectBeat * p.headDim.U + dimension.U\n    projectionInput(dimension) := contextMemory(contextIndex)\n  }\n\n  outputProjection.io.in.valid := state === sProjectFeed\n  outputProjection.io.in.bits.data := projectionInput.asUInt\n  outputProjection.io.in.bits.st := projectBeat === 0.U\n  outputProjection.io.in.bits.addr := projectToken * p.qHeads.U + projectBeat\n  outputProjection.io.in.bits.last := projectBeat === (p.qHeads - 1).U\n\n  when(outputProjection.io.in.fire) {\n    when(projectBeat === (p.qHeads - 1).U) {\n      projectBeat := 0.U\n      state := sProjectDrain\n    }.otherwise {\n      projectBeat := projectBeat + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  io.out.valid := outputProjection.io.out.valid && state === sProjectDrain\n  io.out.bits := outputProjection.io.out.bits\n  outputProjection.io.out.ready := io.out.ready && state === sProjectDrain\n\n  when(io.out.fire && io.out.bits.last) {\n    when(projectToken === seqLimit - 1.U) {\n      projectToken := 0.U\n      collectToken := 0.U\n      state := sCollect\n    }.otherwise {\n      projectToken := projectToken + 1.U\n      projectBeat := 0.U\n      state := sProjectFeed\n    }\n  }\n\n  // The captured reference uses no explicit attention mask and applies the\n  // immutable headDim^-0.5 scaling. Legacy quantization/configuration ports are\n  // retained for ABI compatibility but cannot select another model convention.\n  dontTouch(io.mask)\n  dontTouch(io.scoreScale)\n  dontTouch(io.ctxInvScale)\n  dontTouch(io.ctxZeroPoint)\n  dontTouch(io.outInvScale)\n}\n\nclass AttentionMHA(p: AttentionParams) extends Attention(p)\nclass AttentionGQA(p: AttentionParams) extends Attention(p)\nclass AttentionMQA(p: AttentionParams) extends Attention(p)\n",

      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\nimport hardfloat._\nimport hardfloat.consts\n\nobject AccMath {\n  def ceilDiv(a: Int, b: Int): Int = (a + b - 1) / b\n\n  def resizeSigned(x: SInt, width: Int): SInt = resizeSignedToUInt(x, width).asSInt\n\n  def resizeSignedToUInt(x: SInt, width: Int): UInt = {\n    val u = x.asUInt\n    if (width <= x.getWidth) {\n      u(width - 1, 0)\n    } else {\n      Cat(Fill(width - x.getWidth, u(x.getWidth - 1)), u)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nobject IeeeMath {\n  private val fp16ExpWidth = 5\n  private val fp16SigWidth = 11\n  private val fp32ExpWidth = 8\n  private val fp32SigWidth = 24\n\n  val fp32Zero: UInt = 0.U(32.W)\n  val fp32One: UInt = \"h3f800000\".U(32.W)\n\n  def fp32Constant(value: Double): UInt = {\n    val bits = java.lang.Float.floatToRawIntBits(value.toFloat)\n    BigInt(bits.toLong & 0xffffffffL).U(32.W)\n  }\n\n  def toFp32(value: UInt, bits: Int): UInt = {\n    require(bits == 16 || bits == 32, s\"IEEE input width must be 16 or 32, got $bits\")\n    if (bits == 32) {\n      value\n    } else {\n      val convert = Module(new RecFNToRecFN(fp16ExpWidth, fp16SigWidth, fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth))\n      convert.io.in := recFNFromFN(fp16ExpWidth, fp16SigWidth, value)\n      convert.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n      convert.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n      fNFromRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, convert.io.out)\n    }\n  }\n\n  def fromFp32(value: UInt, bits: Int): UInt = {\n    require(bits == 16 || bits == 32, s\"IEEE output width must be 16 or 32, got $bits\")\n    if (bits == 32) {\n      value\n    } else {\n      val convert = Module(new RecFNToRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, fp16ExpWidth, fp16SigWidth))\n      convert.io.in := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, value)\n      convert.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n      convert.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n      fNFromRecFN(fp16ExpWidth, fp16SigWidth, convert.io.out)\n    }\n  }\n\n  def addFp32(a: UInt, b: UInt): UInt = {\n    val add = Module(new AddRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth))\n    add.io.subOp := false.B\n    add.io.a := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, a)\n    add.io.b := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, b)\n    add.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n    add.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n    fNFromRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, add.io.out)\n  }\n\n  def subFp32(a: UInt, b: UInt): UInt = {\n    val sub = Module(new AddRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth))\n    sub.io.subOp := true.B\n    sub.io.a := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, a)\n    sub.io.b := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, b)\n    sub.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n    sub.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n    fNFromRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, sub.io.out)\n  }\n\n  def mulFp32(a: UInt, b: UInt): UInt = {\n    val mul = Module(new MulRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth))\n    mul.io.a := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, a)\n    mul.io.b := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, b)\n    mul.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n    mul.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n    fNFromRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, mul.io.out)\n  }\n\n  def lessThanFp32(a: UInt, b: UInt): Bool = {\n    val compare = Module(new CompareRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth))\n    compare.io.a := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, a)\n    compare.io.b := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, b)\n    compare.io.signaling := false.B\n    compare.io.lt\n  }\n\n  def sumFp32(values: Seq[UInt]): UInt = {\n    require(values.nonEmpty, \"sumFp32 requires at least one value\")\n    values.reduce((a, b) => addFp32(a, b))\n  }\n}\n\nfinal case class StreamSpec(dataBits: Int, addrBits: Int) {\n  require(dataBits > 0, \"dataBits must be positive\")\n  require(addrBits > 0, \"addrBits must be positive\")\n}\n\nclass StageConfig(seqBits: Int) extends Bundle {\n  val seqlen = UInt(seqBits.W)\n  val prefill = Bool()\n  val singleQuery = Bool()\n}\n\nclass StreamBeat(spec: StreamSpec) extends Bundle {\n  val data = UInt(spec.dataBits.W)\n  val st = Bool()\n  val addr = UInt(spec.addrBits.W)\n  val last = Bool()\n}\n\nclass WeightWrite(dataBits: Int, addrBits: Int) extends Bundle {\n  val addr = UInt(addrBits.W)\n  val data = UInt(dataBits.W)\n}\n\nclass Int8QuantPorts extends Bundle {\n  val outInvScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n  val outZeroPoint = Input(SInt(8.W))\n}\n\nclass LinearScalePorts extends Bundle {\n  val outScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n  val biasScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n}\n",

ResidualParams(hiddenSize, lanes = lanes, elemBits = outputBits, batchSize = batchSize, maxSeqLen = maxSeqLen)\n  val ffn = DenseFFNParams(hiddenSize, intermediateSize, lanes = lanes, elemBits = StreamBeat(p.inSpec)))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(p.inSpec))\n  })\n\n  val rmsIn = Module(new RMSNorm(p.rmsIn))\n  val qkv = Module(new QKVProjection(p.qkv))\n  val qkNorm = Module(new QKNorm(p.qkNorm))\n  val rope = Module(new RoPE(p.rope))\n  val attn = Module(new AttentionMQA(p.attn))\n  val outProj = Module(new Linear(p.outLinear))\n  val postAttnNorm = Module(new RMSNorm(p.rmsFp))\n  val add1 = Module(new ResidualAdd(p.residual))\n  val preFfnNorm = Module(new RMSNorm(p.rmsIn))\n  val mlp = Module(new GatedMLP(p.mlp))\n  val postFfnNorm = Module(new RMSNorm(p.rmsFp))\n  val add2 = Module(new ResidualAdd(p.residual))\n\n  val res1Q = Module(new Queue(new StreamBeat(p.inSpec), p.batchSize * p.rmsIn.vector.beats))\n  val res2Q = Module(new Queue(new StreamBeat(p.inSpec), p.batchSize * p.rmsIn.vector.beats))\n\n  io.in.ready := rmsIn.io.in.ready && res1Q.io.enq.ready\n  rmsIn.io.in.valid := io.in.valid && res1Q.io.enq.ready\n  rmsIn.io.in.bits := io.in.bits\n  res1Q.io.enq.valid := io.in.valid && rmsIn.io.in.ready\n  res1Q.io.enq.bits := io.in.bits\n\n  rmsIn.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  rmsIn.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  rmsIn.io.weight.valid := false.B\n  rmsIn.io.weight.bits := 0.U\n  rmsIn.io.quant.outInvScale := 0.U\n  rmsIn.io.quant.outZeroPoint := 0.S\n\n  qkv.io.start := io.start\n  qkv.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  qkv.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  qkv.io.weight.valid := false.B\n  qkv.io.weight.bits := 0.U.asTypeOf(qkv.io.weight.bits)\n  qkv.io.bias.valid := false.B\n  qkv.io.bias.bits := 0.U.asTypeOf(qkv.io.bias.bits)\n  qkv.io.qScale := 0.U\n  qkv.io.kScale := 0.U\n  qkv.io.vScale := 0.U\n  qkv.io.in <> rmsIn.io.out\n\n  qkNorm.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  qkNorm.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  qkNorm.io.qWeight.valid := false.B\n  qkNorm.io.qWeight.bits := 0.U\n  qkNorm.io.kWeight.valid := false.B\n  qkNorm.io.kWeight.bits := 0.U\n  qkNorm.io.in <> qkv.io.out\n\n  rope.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  rope.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  rope.io.position := io.position\n  rope.io.in <> qkNorm.io.out\n\n  attn.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  attn.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  attn.io.mask := 0.U\n  attn.io.scoreScale := 0.U\n  attn.io.ctxInvScale := 0.U\n  attn.io.ctxZeroPoint := 0.U\n  attn.io.outInvScale := 0.U\n  attn.io.in <> rope.io.out\n\n  outProj.io.start := io.start\n  outProj.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  outProj.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  outProj.io.weight.valid := false.B\n  outProj.io.weight.bits := 0.U.asTypeOf(outProj.io.weight.bits)\n  outProj.io.bias.valid := false.B\n  outProj.io.bias.bits := 0.U.asTypeOf(outProj.io.bias.bits)\n  outProj.io.scale.outScale := 0.U\n  outProj.io.scale.biasScale := 0.U\n  outProj.io.in.valid := attn.io.out.valid\n  outProj.io.in.bits.data := attn.io.out.bits.data\n  outProj.io.in.bits.st := attn.io.out.bits.st\n  outProj.io.in.bits.addr := attn.io.out.bits.addr\n  outProj.io.in.bits.last := attn.io.out.bits.last\n  attn.io.out.ready := outProj.io.in.ready\n\n  postAttnNorm.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  postAttnNorm.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  postAttnNorm.io.weight.valid := false.B\n  postAttnNorm.io.weight.bits := 0.U\n  postAttnNorm.io.quant.outInvScale := 0.U\n  postAttnNorm.io.quant.outZeroPoint := 0.S\n  postAttnNorm.io.in <> outProj.io.out\n\n  add1.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  add1.io.residual <> res1Q.io.deq\n  add1.io.computed <> postAttnNorm.io.out\n\n  add1.io.out.ready := preFfnNorm.io.in.ready && res2Q.io.enq.ready\n  preFfnNorm.io.in.valid := add1.io.out.valid && res2Q.io.enq.ready\n  preFfnNorm.io.in.bits := add1.io.out.bits\n  res2Q.io.enq.valid := add1.io.out.valid && preFfnNorm.io.in.ready\n  res2Q.io.enq.bits := add1.io.out.bits\n\n  preFfnNorm.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  preFfnNorm.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  preFfnNorm.io.weight.valid := false.B\n  preFfnNorm.io.weight.bits := 0.U\n  preFfnNorm.io.quant.outInvScale := 0.U\n  preFfnNorm.io.quant.outZeroPoint := 0.S\n\n  mlp.io.start := io.start\n  mlp.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  mlp.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  mlp.io.gateWeight.valid := false.B\n  mlp.io.gateWeight.bits := 0.U.asTypeOf(mlp.io.gateWeight.bits)\n  mlp.io.gateBias.valid := false.B\n  mlp.io.gateBias.bits := 0.U.asTypeOf(mlp.io.gateBias.bits)\n  mlp.io.upWeight.valid := false.B\n  mlp.io.upWeight.bits := 0.U.asTypeOf(mlp.io.upWeight.bits)\n mlp.io.downBias.valid := false.B\n  mlp.io.downBias.bits := 0.U.asTypeOf(mlp.io.downBias.bits)\n  mlp.io.in <> preFfnNorm.io.out\n\n  postFfnNorm.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  postFfnNorm.io.cfgValid :=       "bytes": 1823,
      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class ElementwiseMulParams(\n  hiddenSize: Int,\n  lanes: Int = 8,\n  elemBits: Int = 16,\n  outputBits: Int = 16,\n  batchSize: Int = 16,\n  maxSeqLen: Int = 16\n) {\n  require(hiddenSize % lanes == 0, \"hiddenSize must be divisible by lanes\")\n  require(elemBits == 16 || elemBits == 32, \"ElementwiseMul inputs must be IEEE FP16 or FP32\")\n  require(outputBits == 16 || outputBits == 32, \"ElementwiseMul output must be IEEE FP16 or FP32\")\n  val beats: Int = hiddenSize / lanes\n  val inputBeatBits: Int = lanes * elemBits\n  val outputBeatBits: Int = lanes * outputBits\n  val addrBits: Int = log2Ceil(batchSize * beats max 2)\n}\n\nclass ElementwiseMul(p: ElementwiseMulParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val lhs = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.inputBeatBits, p.addrBits))))\n    val rhs = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.inputBeatBits, p.addrBits))))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.outputBeatBits, p.addrBits)))\n  })\n\n  io.lhs.ready := io.rhs.valid && io.out.ready\n  io.rhs.ready := io.lhs.valid && io.out.ready\n  io.out.valid := io.lhs.valid && io.rhs.valid\n\n  val a = io.lhs.bits.data.asTypeOf(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val b = io.rhs.bits.data.asTypeOf(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val y = Wire(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.outputBits.W)))\n  for (i <- 0 until p.lanes) {\n    val product = IeeeMath.mulFp32(\n      IeeeMath.toFp32(a(i), p.elemBits),\n      IeeeMath.toFp32(b(i), p.elemBits)\n    )\n    y(i) := IeeeMath.fromFp32(product, p.outputBits)\n  }\n\n  io.out.bits.data := y.asUInt\n  io.out.bits.st := io.lhs.bits.st\n  io.out.bits.addr := io.lhs.bits.addr\n  io.out.bits.last := io.lhs.bits.last\n}\n",
      "path": "accagent/framework/templates/operator_chisel/Elementwise.scala",

      "bytes": 6269,
      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class DenseFFNParams(\n  hiddenSize: Int,\n  intermediateSize: Int,\n  lanes: Int = 12,\n  elemBits: Int = 8,\n  outputBits: Int = 32,\n  tileM: Int = 1,\n  tileN: Int = 64,\n  tileK: Int = 64,\n  batchSize: Int = 16,\n  maxSeqLen: Int = 16,\n  activation: String = \"relu\"\n) {\n  require(tileM > 0 && tileN > 0 && tileK > 0, \"FFN tile parameters must be positive\")\n  val up = LinearParams(hiddenSize, intermediateSize, inLanes = lanes, outLanes = lanes, elemBits = elemBits, outputBits = elemBits, batchSize = batchSize, maxSeqLen = maxSeqLen, hasBias = true, fusedActivation = activation)\n  val down = LinearParams(intermediateSize, hiddenSize, inLanes = lanes, outLanes = lanes, elemBits = elemBits, outputBits = outputBits, batchSize = batchSize, maxSeqLen = maxSeqLen, hasBias = true)\n}\n\nclass DenseFFN(p: DenseFFNParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val start = Input(Bool())\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val upWeight = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.up.weightTileBits, p.up.weightAddrBits)))\n    val upBias = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.up.biasBeatBits, log2Ceil(p.up.outBeats max 2))))\n    val downWeight = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.down.weightTileBits, p.down.weightAddrBits)))\n    val downBias = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.down.biasBeatBits, log2Ceil(p.down.outBeats max 2))))\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.up.inputBeatBits, p.up.inputAddrBits))))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.down.outputBeatBits, p.down.outputAddrBits)))\n  })\n\n  val up = Module(new Linear(p.up))\n  val down = Module(new Linear(p.down))\n  val q = Module(new Queue(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.up.outputBeatBits, p.up.outputAddrBits)), 2))\n\n  up.io.start := io.start\n  up.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  up.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  up.io.weight <> io.upWeight\n  up.io.bias <> io.upBias\n  up.io.scale.outScale := 0.U\n  up.io.scale.biasScale := 0.U\n  up.io.in <> io.in\n  up.io.out <> q.io.enq\n\n  down.io.start := io.start\n  down.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  down.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  down.io.weight <> io.downWeight\n  down.io.bias <> io.downBias\n  down.io.scale.outScale := 0.U\n  down.io.scale.biasScale := 0.U\n  down.io.in <> q.io.deq\n  down.io.out <> io.out\n}\n\nclass DenseMLP(p: DenseFFNParams) extends DenseFFN(p)\n\nfinal case class GatedMLPParams(\n  hiddenSize: Int,\n  intermediateSize: Int,\n  lanes: Int = 12,\n  elemBits: Int = 8,\n  outputBits: Int = 32,\n  tileM: Int = 1,\n  tileN: Int = 64,\n  tileK: Int = 64,\n  batchSize: Int = 16,\n  maxSeqLen: Int = 16,\n  activation: String = \"silu\",\n  hasBias: Boolean = false\n) {\n  require(tileM > 0 && tileN > 0 && tileK > 0, \"GatedMLP tile parameters must be positive\")\n  val gate = LinearParams(hiddenSize, intermediateSize, inLanes = lanes, outLanes = lanes, elemBits = elemBits, outputBits = elemBits, batchSize = batchSize, maxSeqLen = maxSeqLen, hasBias = hasBias)\n  val up = LinearParams(hiddenSize, intermediateSize, inLanes = lanes, outLanes = lanes, elemBits = elemBits, outputBits = elemBits, batchSize = batchSize, maxSeqLen = maxSeqLen, hasBias = hasBias)\n  val act = ActivationParams(intermediateSize, lanes = lanes, elemBits = elemBits, batchSize = batchSize, maxSeqLen = maxSeqLen, kind = activation)\n  val mul = ElementwiseMulParams(intermediateSize, lanes = lanes, elemBits = elemBits, outputBits = elemBits, batchSize = batchSize, maxSeqLen = maxSeqLen)\n  val down = LinearParams(intermediateSize, hiddenSize, inLanes = lanes, outLanes = lanes, elemBits = elemBits, outputBits = outputBits, batchSize = batchSize, maxSeqLen = maxSeqLen, hasBias = hasBias)\n}\n\nclass GatedMLP(p: GatedMLPParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val start = Input(Bool())\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val gateWeight = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.gate.weightTileBits, p.gate.weightAddrBits)))\n    val gateBias = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.gate.biasBeatBits, log2Ceil(p.gate.outBeats max 2))))\n    val upWeight = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.up.weightTileBits, p.up.weightAddrBits)))\n    val upBias = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.up.biasBeatBits, log2Ceil(p.up.outBeats max 2))))\n    val downWeight = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.down.weightTileBits, p.down.weightAddrBits)))\n    val downBias = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.down.biasBeatBits, log2Ceil(p.down.outBeats max 2))))\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.gate.inputBeatBits, p.gate.inputAddrBits))))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.down.outputBeatBits, p.down.outputAddrBits)))\n  })\n\n  val gate = Module(new Linear(p.gate))\n  val up = Module(new Linear(p.up))\n  val act = Module(new Activation(p.act))\n  val actQ = Module(new Queue(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.mul.inputBeatBits, p.mul.addrBits)), 4))\n  val upQ = Module(new Queue(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.mul.inputBeatBits, p.mul.addrBits)), 4))\n  val mul = Module(new ElementwiseMul(p.mul))\n  val down = Module(new Linear(p.down))\n\n  gate.io.start := io.start\n  gate.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  gate.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  gate.io.weight <> io.gateWeight\n  gate.io.bias <> io.gateBias\n  gate.io.scale.outScale := 0.U\n  gate.io.scale.biasScale := 0.U\n\n  up.io.start := io.start\n  up.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  up.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  up.io.weight <> io.upWeight\n  up.io.bias <> io.upBias\n  up.io.scale.outScale := 0.U\n  up.io.scale.biasScale := 0.U\n\n  io.in.ready := gate.io.in.ready && up.io.in.ready\n  gate.io.in.valid := io.in.valid && up.io.in.ready\n  gate.io.in.bits := io.in.bits\n  up.io.in.valid := io.in.valid && gate.io.in.ready\n  up.io.in.bits := io.in.bits\n\n  act.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  act.io.in <> gate.io.out\n  act.io.out <> actQ.io.enq\n\n  mul.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  up.io.out <> upQ.io.enq\n  mul.io.lhs <> actQ.io.deq\n  mul.io.rhs <> upQ.io.deq\n\n  down.io.start := io.start\n  down.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  down.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  down.io.weight <> io.downWeight\n  down.io.bias <> io.downBias\n  down.io.scale.outScale := 0.U\n  down.io.scale.biasScale := 0.U\n  down.io.in <> mul.io.out\n  down.io.out <> io.out\n}\n",

      "bytes": 1914,
      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class KVCacheParams(\n  kvHeads: Int,\n  headDim: Int,\n  seqLen: Int,\n  elemsPerBeat: Int = 2,\n  elemBits: Int = 8,\n  batchSize: Int = 16\n) {\n  require(kvHeads > 0, \"kvHeads must be positive\")\n  require(headDim % elemsPerBeat == 0, \"headDim must be divisible by elemsPerBeat\")\n  val headBeats: Int = headDim / elemsPerBeat\n  val depth: Int = batchSize * kvHeads * seqLen * headBeats\n  val beatBits: Int = elemsPerBeat * elemBits\n  val addrBits: Int = log2Ceil(depth max 2)\n}\n\nclass KVCache(p: KVCacheParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val write = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.beatBits, p.addrBits))))\n    val readAddr = Flipped(Decoupled(UInt(p.addrBits.W)))\n    val read = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.beatBits, p.addrBits)))\n  })\n\n  val dataMem = Reg(Vec(p.depth, UInt(p.beatBits.W)))\n  val stMem = RegInit(VecInit(Seq.fill(p.depth)(false.B)))\n  val lastMem = RegInit(VecInit(Seq.fill(p.depth)(false.B)))\n  val readData = Reg(UInt(p.beatBits.W))\n  val readAddrReg = Reg(UInt(p.addrBits.W))\n  val readSt = RegInit(false.B)\n  val readLast = RegInit(false.B)\n  val readValid = RegInit(false.B)\n\n  io.write.ready := true.B\n  when(io.write.fire) {\n    dataMem(io.write.bits.addr) := io.write.bits.data\n    stMem(io.write.bits.addr) := io.write.bits.st\n    lastMem(io.write.bits.addr) := io.write.bits.last\n  }\n\n  io.readAddr.ready := !readValid || io.read.ready\n  when(io.readAddr.fire) {\n    readData := dataMem(io.readAddr.bits)\n    readAddrReg := io.readAddr.bits\n    readSt := stMem(io.readAddr.bits)\n    readLast := lastMem(io.readAddr.bits)\n    readValid := true.B\n  }\n  when(io.read.fire) {\n    readValid := false.B\n  }\n\n  io.read.valid := readValid\n  io.read.bits.data := readData\n  io.read.bits.st := readSt\n  io.read.bits.addr := readAddrReg\n  io.read.bits.last := readLast\n}\n",
      "path": "accagent/framework/templates/operator_chisel/KVCache.scala",
      "sha256": "a179ec331fba4025c1b33f88d122fdd06f9f9a6bc50e2bf7d50ec570b12b3136",

      "bytes": 6281,
      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class LinearParams(\n  inDim: Int,\n  outDim: Int,\n  inLanes: Int = 8,\n  outLanes: Int = 8,\n  elemBits: Int = 16,\n  accBits: Int = 32,\n  outputBits: Int = 16,\n  batchSize: Int = 16,\n  maxSeqLen: Int = 16,\n  hasBias: Boolean = true,\n  fusedActivation: String = \"none\"\n) {\n  require(inDim > 0 && outDim > 0, \"linear dimensions must be positive\")\n  require(inDim % inLanes == 0, \"inDim must be divisible by inLanes\")\n  require(outDim % outLanes == 0, \"outDim must be divisible by outLanes\")\n  require(elemBits == 16 || elemBits == 32, \"Linear inputs and weights must be IEEE FP16 or FP32\")\n  require(accBits == 32, \"Linear accumulation must use FP32\")\n  require(outputBits == 16 || outputBits == 32, \"Linear output must be IEEE FP16 or FP32\")\n  require(fusedActivation == \"none\" || fusedActivation == \"relu\", \"unsupported fused activation\")\n  val inBeats: Int = inDim / inLanes\n  val outBeats: Int = outDim / outLanes\n  val inputBeatBits: Int = inLanes * elemBits\n  val outputBeatBits: Int = outLanes * outputBits\n  val weightTileBits: Int = inLanes * outLanes * elemBits\n  val weightDepth: Int = inBeats * outBeats\n  val inputAddrBits: Int = log2Ceil(batchSize * inBeats max 2)\n  val outputAddrBits: Int = log2Ceil(batchSize * outBeats max 2)\n  val weightAddrBits: Int = log2Ceil(weightDepth max 2)\n  val biasBeatBits: Int = outLanes * accBits\n}\n\nclass Linear(p: LinearParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val start = Input(Bool())\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val weight = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.weightTileBits, p.weightAddrBits)))\n    val bias = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.biasBeatBits, log2Ceil(p.outBeats max 2))))\n    val scale = new LinearScalePorts\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.inputBeatBits, p.inputAddrBits))))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.outputBeatBits, p.outputAddrBits)))\n  })\n\n  val inputMem = Reg(Vec(p.inBeats, UInt(p.inputBeatBits.W)))\n  val weightMem = Reg(Vec(p.weightDepth, UInt(p.weightTileBits.W)))\n  val biasMem = Reg(Vec(p.outBeats, UInt(p.biasBeatBits.W)))\n\n  val weightLoadCnt = RegInit(0.U(p.weightAddrBits.W))\n  val weightLoaded = RegInit(false.B)\n  io.weight.ready := !weightLoaded && io.weight.bits.addr === weightLoadCnt\n  when(io.weight.fire) {\n    weightMem(weightLoadCnt) := io.weight.bits.data\n    when(weightLoadCnt === (p.weightDepth - 1).U) {\n      weightLoaded := true.B\n      weightLoadCnt := 0.U\n    }.otherwise {\n      weightLoadCnt := weightLoadCnt + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  private val biasAddrBits = log2Ceil(p.outBeats max 2)\n  val biasLoadCnt = RegInit(0.U(biasAddrBits.W))\n  val biasLoaded = RegInit((!p.hasBias).B)\n  if (p.hasBias) {\n    io.bias.ready := !biasLoaded && io.bias.bits.addr === biasLoadCnt\n    when(io.bias.fire) {\n      biasMem(biasLoadCnt) := io.bias.bits.data\n      when(biasLoadCnt === (p.outBeats - 1).U) {\n        biasLoaded := true.B\n        biasLoadCnt := 0.U\n      }.otherwise {\n        biasLoadCnt := biasLoadCnt + 1.U\n      }\n    }\n  } else {\n    io.bias.ready := false.B\n  }\n\n  val parametersLoaded = weightLoaded && biasLoaded\n  val sLoad :: sPrep :: sCompute :: sEmit :: Nil = Enum(4)\n  val state = RegInit(sLoad)\n  val inCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.inBeats max 2).W))\n  val rowCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.inBeats max 2).W))\n  val outCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.outBeats max 2).W))\n  val tokenCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.batchSize max 2).W))\n  val vectorStart = RegInit(false.B)\n  val acc = Reg(Vec(p.outLanes, UInt(32.W)))\n\n  when(io.start && parametersLoaded) {\n    state := sLoad\n    inCnt := 0.U\n    rowCnt := 0.U\n    outCnt := 0.U\n    tokenCnt := 0.U\n  }\n\n  io.in.ready := state === sLoad && parametersLoaded\n  when(io.in.fire) {\n    inputMem(inCnt) := io.in.bits.data\n    when(inCnt === 0.U) {\n      vectorStart := io.in.bits.st\n    }\n    when(inCnt === (p.inBeats - 1).U) {\n      inCnt := 0.U\n      outCnt := 0.U\n      state := sPrep\n    }.otherwise {\n      inCnt := inCnt + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  val biasVec = biasMem(outCnt).asTypeOf(Vec(p.outLanes, UInt(32.W)))\n  when(state === sPrep) {\n    for (o <- 0 until p.outLanes) {\n      if (p.hasBias) {\n        acc(o) := biasVec(o)\n      } else {\n        acc(o) := IeeeMath.fp32Zero\n      }\n    }\n    rowCnt := 0.U\n    state := sCompute\n  }\n\n  val inVec = inputMem(rowCnt).asTypeOf(Vec(p.inLanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val weightAddr = (outCnt * p.inBeats.U + rowCnt)(p.weightAddrBits - 1, 0)\n  val weightTile = weightMem(weightAddr).asTypeOf(Vec(p.outLanes, Vec(p.inLanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W))))\n  val dotProducts = Wire(Vec(p.outLanes, UInt(32.W)))\n  for (o <- 0 until p.outLanes) {\n    val products = (0 until p.inLanes).map { i =>\n      val inputFp32 = IeeeMath.toFp32(inVec(i), p.elemBits)\n      val weightFp32 = IeeeMath.toFp32(weightTile(o)(i), p.elemBits)\n      IeeeMath.mulFp32(inputFp32, weightFp32)\n    }\n    dotProducts(o) := IeeeMath.sumFp32(products)\n  }\n\n  when(state === sCompute) {\n    for (o <- 0 until p.outLanes) {\n      acc(o) := IeeeMath.addFp32(acc(o), dotProducts(o))\n    }\n    when(rowCnt === (p.inBeats - 1).U) {\n      state := sEmit\n    }.otherwise {\n      rowCnt := rowCnt + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  val outVec = Wire(Vec(p.outLanes, UInt(p.outputBits.W)))\n  for (o <- 0 until p.outLanes) {\n    val activated = if (p.fusedActivation == \"relu\") {\n      Mux(IeeeMath.lessThanFp32(acc(o), IeeeMath.fp32Zero), IeeeMath.fp32Zero, acc(o))\n    } else {\n      acc(o)\n    }\n    outVec(o) := IeeeMath.fromFp32(activated, p.outputBits)\n  }\n\n  io.out.valid := state === sEmit\n  io.out.bits.data := outVec.asUInt\n  io.out.bits.st := vectorStart && outCnt === 0.U\n  io.out.bits.addr := tokenCnt * p.outBeats.U + outCnt\n  io.out.bits.last := outCnt === (p.outBeats - 1).U\n\n  when(io.out.fire) {\n    when(outCnt === (p.outBeats - 1).U) {\n      state := sLoad\n      outCnt := 0.U\n      tokenCnt := Mux(tokenCnt === (p.batchSize - 1).U, 0.U, tokenCnt + 1.U)\n    }.otherwise {\n      outCnt := outCnt + 1.U\n      state := sPrep\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nclass ParametricLinearInt8ToInt8(p: LinearParams) extends Linear(p)\n\nclass ParametricLinearInt8ToFP32(p: LinearParams)\n    extends Linear(p.copy(outputBits = 32))\n",

      "bytes": 1419,
      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class AttentionMaskParams(\n  seqLen: Int,\n  causal: Boolean = true,\n  slidingWindow: Int = 0,\n  globalEvery: Int = 0\n) {\n  require(seqLen > 0, \"seqLen must be positive\")\n  require(slidingWindow >= 0, \"slidingWindow must be non-negative\")\n  require(globalEvery >= 0, \"globalEvery must be non-negative\")\n  val seqBits: Int = log2Ceil(seqLen + 1 max 2)\n  val rowBits: Int = log2Ceil(seqLen max 2)\n}\n\nclass AttentionMask(p: AttentionMaskParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(p.seqBits))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val rowIndex = Input(UInt(p.rowBits.W))\n    val mask = Output(UInt(p.seqLen.W))\n  })\n\n  val seqLimit = RegInit(p.seqLen.U(p.seqBits.W))\n  when(io.cfgValid) {\n    seqLimit := Mux(io.cfg.seqlen === 0.U || io.cfg.seqlen > p.seqLen.U, p.seqLen.U, io.cfg.seqlen)\n  }\n\n  val bits = Wire(Vec(p.seqLen, Bool()))\n  val globalRow =\n    if (p.globalEvery > 0) io.rowIndex % p.globalEvery.U === 0.U else false.B\n  for (i <- 0 until p.seqLen) {\n    val idx = i.U(p.rowBits.W)\n    val active = i.U < seqLimit\n    val causalKeep = if (p.causal) idx <= io.rowIndex else true.B\n    val slidingKeep =\n      if (p.slidingWindow > 0) (idx + p.slidingWindow.U) > io.rowIndex else true.B\n    bits(i) := active && causalKeep && (slidingKeep || globalRow)\n  }\n  io.mask := bits.asUInt\n}\n",
      "path": "accagent/framework/templates/operator_chisel/Mask.scala",
      "sha256": "bfb70e48641d60c25100b8240d5fef461aa954847c5fe5777b7fbb5445fc0e0a",
      "truncated": false

      "bytes": 8824,
      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\nimport hardfloat._\nimport hardfloat.consts\n\nfinal case class VectorNormParams(\n  hiddenSize: Int,\n  lanes: Int,\n  inputBits: Int = 32,\n  outputBits: Int = 16,\n  batchSize: Int = 16,\n  maxSeqLen: Int = 16,\n  hasMeanSub: Boolean = true,\n  hasBeta: Boolean = true,\n  eps: Double = 1.0e-5\n) {\n  require(hiddenSize > 0, \"hiddenSize must be positive\")\n  require(hiddenSize % lanes == 0, \"hiddenSize must be divisible by lanes\")\n  require(inputBits == 16 || inputBits == 32, \"VectorNorm input and affine weights must be FP16 or FP32\")\n  require(outputBits == 16 || outputBits == 32, \"VectorNorm output must be FP16 or FP32\")\n  require(eps > 0.0, \"normalization epsilon must be positive\")\n  val beats: Int = hiddenSize / lanes\n  val inputBeatBits: Int = lanes * inputBits\n  val outputBeatBits: Int = lanes * outputBits\n  val addrBits: Int = log2Ceil(batchSize * beats max 2)\n  val weightBeats: Int = if (hasBeta) beats * 2 else beats\n}\n\nclass VectorNorm(p: VectorNormParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val weight = Flipped(Decoupled(UInt(p.inputBeatBits.W)))\n    val quant = new Int8QuantPorts\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.inputBeatBits, p.addrBits))))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.outputBeatBits, p.addrBits)))\n  })\n\n  val gamma = Reg(Vec(p.beats, UInt(p.inputBeatBits.W)))\n  val beta = if (p.hasBeta) Some(Reg(Vec(p.beats, UInt(p.inputBeatBits.W)))) else None\n  val weightCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.weightBeats max 2).W))\n  val weightsLoaded = RegInit(false.B)\n\n  io.weight.ready := !weightsLoaded\n  when(io.weight.fire) {\n    if (p.hasBeta) {\n      when(weightCnt < p.beats.U) {\n        beta.get(weightCnt) := io.weight.bits\n      }.otherwise {\n        gamma(weightCnt - p.beats.U) := io.weight.bits\n      }\n    } else {\n      gamma(weightCnt) := io.weight.bits\n    }\n    when(weightCnt === (p.weightBeats - 1).U) {\n      weightCnt := 0.U\n      weightsLoaded := true.B\n    }.otherwise {\n      weightCnt := weightCnt + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  val inputData = Reg(Vec(p.beats, UInt(p.inputBeatBits.W)))\n  val inputStart = Reg(Vec(p.beats, Bool()))\n  val inputAddr = Reg(Vec(p.beats, UInt(p.addrBits.W)))\n  val inputLast = Reg(Vec(p.beats, Bool()))\n\n  val sLoad :: sPrepare :: sSqrtIssue :: sSqrtWait :: sRecipIssue :: sRecipWait :: sEmit :: Nil = Enum(7)\n  val state = RegInit(sLoad)\n  val inputCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.beats max 2).W))\n  val outputCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.beats max 2).W))\n  val sumReg = Reg(UInt(32.W))\n  val squareSumReg = Reg(UInt(32.W))\n  val meanReg = Reg(UInt(32.W))\n  val varianceReg = Reg(UInt(32.W))\n  val denominatorReg = Reg(UInt(32.W))\n  val inverseReg = Reg(UInt(32.W))\n\n  io.in.ready := state === sLoad && weightsLoaded\n  val incoming = io.in.bits.data.asTypeOf(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.inputBits.W)))\n  val incomingFp32 = (0 until p.lanes).map(i => IeeeMath.toFp32(incoming(i), p.inputBits))\n  val incomingSquares = incomingFp32.map(x => IeeeMath.mulFp32(x, x))\n  val beatSum = IeeeMath.sumFp32(incomingFp32)\n  val beatSquareSum = IeeeMath.sumFp32(incomingSquares)\n\n  when(io.in.fire) {\n    inputData(inputCnt) := io.in.bits.data\n    inputStart(inputCnt) := io.in.bits.st\n    inputAddr(inputCnt) := io.in.bits.addr\n    inputLast(inputCnt) := io.in.bits.last\n    when(inputCnt === 0.U) {\n      sumReg := beatSum\n      squareSumReg := beatSquareSum\n    }.otherwise {\n      sumReg := IeeeMath.addFp32(sumReg, beatSum)\n      squareSumReg := IeeeMath.addFp32(squareSumReg, beatSquareSum)\n    }\n    when(inputCnt === (p.beats - 1).U) {\n      inputCnt := 0.U\n      state := sPrepare\n    }.otherwise {\n      inputCnt := inputCnt + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  val reciprocalHiddenSize = IeeeMath.fp32Constant(1.0 / p.hiddenSize.toDouble)\n  val meanValue = IeeeMath.mulFp32(sumReg, reciprocalHiddenSize)\n  val meanSquareValue = IeeeMath.mulFp32(squareSumReg, reciprocalHiddenSize)\n  val centeredVariance = if (p.hasMeanSub) {\n    val squaredMean = IeeeMath.mulFp32(meanValue, meanValue)\n    val rawVariance = IeeeMath.subFp32(meanSquareValue, squaredMean)\n    Mux(IeeeMath.lessThanFp32(rawVariance, IeeeMath.fp32Zero), IeeeMath.fp32Zero, rawVariance)\n  } else {\n    meanSquareValue\n  }\n  val varianceWithEpsilon = IeeeMath.addFp32(centeredVariance, IeeeMath.fp32Constant(p.eps))\n\n  when(state === sPrepare) {\n    meanReg := (if (p.hasMeanSub) meanValue else IeeeMath.fp32Zero)\n    varianceReg := varianceWithEpsilon\n    state := sSqrtIssue\n  }\n\n  val divSqrt = Module(new DivSqrtRecFN_small(8, 24, 0))\n  divSqrt.io.inValid := state === sSqrtIssue || state === sRecipIssue\n  divSqrt.io.sqrtOp := state === sSqrtIssue\n  divSqrt.io.a := recFNFromFN(8, 24, Mux(state === sSqrtIssue, varianceReg, IeeeMath.fp32One))\n  divSqrt.io.b := recFNFromFN(8, 24, denominatorReg)\n  divSqrt.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n  divSqrt.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n  val divSqrtResult = fNFromRecFN(8, 24, divSqrt.io.out)\n\n  when(state === sSqrtIssue && divSqrt.io.inReady) {\n    state := sSqrtWait\n  }\n  when(state === sSqrtWait && divSqrt.io.outValid_sqrt) {\n    denominatorReg := divSqrtResult\n    state := sRecipIssue\n  }\n  when(state === sRecipIssue && divSqrt.io.inReady) {\n    state := sRecipWait\n  }\n  when(state === sRecipWait && divSqrt.io.outValid_div) {\n    inverseReg := divSqrtResult\n    outputCnt := 0.U\n    state := sEmit\n  }\n\n  val currentInput = inputData(outputCnt).asTypeOf(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.inputBits.W)))\n  val currentGamma = gamma(outputCnt).asTypeOf(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.inputBits.W)))\n  val currentBeta = if (p.hasBeta) {\n    beta.get(outputCnt).asTypeOf(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.inputBits.W)))\n  } else {\n    WireDefault(VecInit(Seq.fill(p.lanes)(0.U(p.inputBits.W))))\n  }\n  val outputData = Wire(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.outputBits.W)))\n  for (lane <- 0 until p.lanes) {\n    val x = IeeeMath.toFp32(currentInput(lane), p.inputBits)\n    val centered = if (p.hasMeanSub) IeeeMath.subFp32(x, meanReg) else x\n    val normalized = IeeeMath.mulFp32(centered, inverseReg)\n    val scaled = IeeeMath.mulFp32(normalized, IeeeMath.toFp32(currentGamma(lane), p.inputBits))\n    val affine = if (p.hasBeta) {\n      IeeeMath.addFp32(scaled, IeeeMath.toFp32(currentBeta(lane), p.inputBits))\n    } else {\n      scaled\n    }\n    outputData(lane) := IeeeMath.fromFp32(affine, p.outputBits)\n  }\n\n  io.out.valid := state === sEmit\n  io.out.bits.data := outputData.asUInt\n  io.out.bits.st := inputStart(outputCnt)\n  io.out.bits.addr := inputAddr(outputCnt)\n  io.out.bits.last := inputLast(outputCnt)\n\n  when(io.out.fire) {\n    when(outputCnt === (p.beats - 1).U) {\n      outputCnt := 0.U\n      state := sLoad\n    }.otherwise {\n      outputCnt := outputCnt + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfinal case class RMSNormParams(\n  hiddenSize: Int,\n  lanes: Int,\n  inputBits: Int = 32,\n  outputBits: Int = 16,\n  batchSize: Int = 16,\n  maxSeqLen: Int = 16,\n  eps: Double = 1.0e-6\n) {\n  val vector = VectorNormParams(\n    hiddenSize = hiddenSize,\n    lanes = lanes,\n    inputBits = inputBits,\n    outputBits = outputBits,\n    batchSize = batchSize,\n    maxSeqLen = maxSeqLen,\n    hasMeanSub = false,\n    hasBeta = false,\n    eps = eps\n  )\n}\n\nclass RMSNorm(p: RMSNormParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.vector.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val weight = Flipped(Decoupled(UInt(p.vector.inputBeatBits.W)))\n    val quant = new Int8QuantPorts\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.vector.inputBeatBits, p.vector.addrBits))))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.vector.outputBeatBits, p.vector.addrBits)))\n  })\n\n  val core = Module(new VectorNorm(p.vector))\n  core.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  core.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  core.io.weight <> io.weight\n  core.io.quant.outInvScale := io.quant.outInvScale\n  core.io.quant.outZeroPoint := io.quant.outZeroPoint\n  core.io.in <> io.in\n  io.out <> core.io.out\n}\n\nclass RMSNormQ(p: RMSNormParams) extends RMSNorm(p)\n\nfinal case class QKNormParams(\n  qkv: QKVProjectionParams,\n  eps: Double = 1.0e-6\n) {\n  val weightBits: Int = qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat * qkv.elemBits\n}\n\nclass QKNorm(p: QKNormParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.qkv.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val qWeight = Flipped(Decoupled(UInt(p.weightBits.W)))\n    val kWeight = Flipped(Decoupled(UInt(p.weightBits.W)))\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new QKVStreamBeat(p.qkv)))\n    val out = Decoupled(new QKVStreamBeat(p.qkv))\n  })\n\n  io.qWeight.ready := true.B\n  io.kWeight.ready := true.B\n  io.in.ready := io.out.ready\n  io.out.valid := io.in.valid\n  io.out.bits := io.in.bits\n}\n",

      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class QKVProjectionParams(\n  hiddenSize: Int,\n  qHeads: Int,\n  kvHeads: Int,\n  headDim: Int,\n  lanes: Int = 8,\n  elemBits: Int = 16,\n  qkvElemsPerBeat: Int = 8,\n  batchSize: Int = 16,\n  maxSeqLen: Int = 16\n) {\n  require(hiddenSize > 0, \"hiddenSize must be positive\")\n  require(qHeads > 0 && kvHeads > 0, \"attention head counts must be positive\")\n  require(qHeads % kvHeads == 0, \"qHeads must be divisible by kvHeads\")\n  require(headDim > 0, \"headDim must be positive\")\n  require(hiddenSize == qHeads * headDim, \"Qwen2 attention input size must equal qHeads * headDim\")\n  require(elemBits == 16, \"the immutable attention boundary uses IEEE FP16 Q/K/V elements\")\n  require(qkvElemsPerBeat > 0, \"qkvElemsPerBeat must be positive\")\n  require(headDim % qkvElemsPerBeat == 0, \"headDim must be divisible by qkvElemsPerBeat\")\n\n  val qDim: Int = qHeads * headDim\n  val kDim: Int = kvHeads * headDim\n  val vDim: Int = kvHeads * headDim\n  val outDim: Int = qDim + kDim + vDim\n  val linear: LinearParams = LinearParams(\n    hiddenSize,\n    outDim,\n",

<action_contract_examples>
[
  {
    "acceptance_checkers": [
      "case_real_weight_artifacts",
      "case_target_model_reference",
      "case_semantic_testbench"
    ],
    "action_type": "verification_evidence_preparation",
    "consumes": [
      "artifact.input.model_config",
      "artifact.input.numeric_policy",
      "artifact.stage3.pipeline_plan",
      "current case-adapter target checkpoint",
      "generated/memory/dut_weight_binding_manifest.json"
    ],
    "id": "example.prepare_real_model_semantic_verification",
    "on_failure": "treat missing reference, explicit tolerance, semantic harness, or DUT weight consumption as a verification-capability/code-generation blocker; do not run a legacy fallback test or promote the layer.",
    "produces": [
      "verification/real_weights/full_tensor_catalog.json",
      "verification/model_reference/reference_manifest.json",
      "verification/semantic_testbench/semantic_testbench_manifest.json",
      "verification/semantic_testbench/dut_weight_binding_requirements.json"
    ],
    "rationale": "Build immutable semantic evidence from the current case adapter before any hardware correctness claim.",
    "requires_approval": false,
    "stage": "verification",
    "tool_roles": [
      "case_weight_manifest_generate",
      "case_target_model_reference",
      "case_semantic_testbench"
    ]
  },
  {
    "acceptance_checkers": [
      "functional_sim",
      "data_order_trace_check",
      "deadlock_watchdog",
      "real_weight_semantic_evidence_check"
    ],
    "action_type": "real_tool_execution",
    "consumes": [
      "artifact.stage6.verification_artifact_contract",
      "verification/model_reference/reference_manifest.json",
      "verification/semantic_testbench/semantic_testbench_manifest.json",
      "generated/memory/dut_weight_binding_manifest.json",
      "verification/board_simulation/board_simulation_manifest.json"
    ],
    "id": "example.run_real_functional_sim",
    "on_failure": "route simulator evidence to Stage8 repair with the violated SACG constraints; do not proceed to Vivado.",
    "produces": [
      "verification/vcs/case_functional_sim.log",
      "verification/board_simulation/rtl_output.memh",
      "verification/debug_closure/boundary_trace.json",
      "verification/case_diagnostics/vcs_functional_diagnosis.json",
      "verification/semantic_evidence/board_axi_ddr.json"
    ],
    "rationale": "Run a real simulator after static hierarchy and artifact gates pass.",
    "requires_approval": false,
    "stage": "verification",
    "tool_roles": [
      "case_vcs_functional_sim",
      "case_vcs_evidence_analyzer",
      "case_board_semantic_evidence"
    ]
  },
  {
    "acceptance_checkers": [
      "planned_checker.formal_axi_property_check"
    ],
    "action_type": "system_capability_gap",
    "consumes": [
      "artifact.stage6.verification_plan"
    ],
    "id": "example.declare_missing_capability",
    "on_failure": "block promotion until the planned checker is implemented or an approved equivalent exists.",
    "produces": [
      "planned checker implementation task"
    ],
    "rationale": "The design team needs a checker not yet implemented by the multi-agent system.",
    "requires_approval": true,
    "stage": "verification",
    "tool_roles": [
      "planned_tool.formal_axi_property_runner"
    ]
  },
  {
    "acceptance_checkers": [
      "backend_app_shell_target_hint_synthesis_check",
      "backend_bounded_recovery_action_check",
      "human_boundary_check"
    ],
    "action_type": "bounded_recovery",
    "consumes": [
      "artifact.stage9.app_shell_integration_contract",
      "artifact.stage9.app_shell_target_selection_decision",
      "backend_board/case_diagnostics/app_shell_target_discovery_after_hint.json"
    ],
    "id": "example.ambiguous_backend_target_recovery",
    "on_failure": "keep runtime bitstream and board runtime blocked until the target contract has cited evidence or explicit approval",
    "produces": [
      "artifact.stage9.backend_bounded_recovery_actions"
    ],
    "rationale": "Real backend evidence produced multiple plausible shell integration targets, so the design team must not guess.",
    "requires_approval": true,
    "stage": "backend_board",
    "tool_roles": [
      "app_shell_target_hint_synthesis",
      "app_shell_target_discovery_after_hint"
    ]
  }
]
</action_contract_examples>
